	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		NOT = {
			any_known_country = {
				has_opinion_modifier = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = cosmo_trader_wives_offer
				}
			}
		}		
	}
	
	immediate = {
			hidden_effect = {
				random_known_country = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = cosmo_traderwife_repeat
					}
					add_opinion = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = cosmo_trader_wives_offer
					}					
				}
				random_known_country = {
				limit = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = cosmo_trader_wives_offer
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = tradecountry
					capital_scope = {
						save_event_target_as = tradecapital						
					}
					add_country_modifier = {
						name = cosmo_trader_marriage
						duration = 10950
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_known_country = {
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = ROOT
								modifier = cosmo_trader_wives_offer
							}
			OR = {
						culture_group = mande		
						culture_group = sahelian

						culture_group = african		
						culture_group = cushitic		
						culture_group = sudanese
			}
						}
						}
						set_ruler_flag = african_trader_wives
					}					
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_known_country = {
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = ROOT
								modifier = cosmo_trader_wives_offer
							}
			OR = {
						culture_group = andean_group

						culture_group = araucanian
			}
						}
						}
						set_ruler_flag = sa_trader_wives
					}
				if = {
					limit = {
						any_known_country = {
							has_opinion_modifier = {
								who = ROOT
								modifier = cosmo_trader_wives_offer
							}
			OR = {
						culture_group = eskaleut		
						culture_group = central_algonquian		
						culture_group = plains_algonquian		
						culture_group = eastern_algonquian		
						culture_group = iroquoian		
						culture_group = siouan		
						culture_group = caddoan		
						culture_group = muskogean
						culture_group = sonoran		
						culture_group = apachean		
						culture_group = na_dene		
						culture_group = penutian
			}
						}
						}
						set_ruler_flag = na_trader_wives
					}					
				}
	}	

	option = {#capital
		name = cosmo_africantrader_events.21.a
		goto = tradecapital
		trigger = {
		event_target:tradecountry = {
			capital_scope = {
				NOT = {
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_trader_wife
				}
			}
		}			
		}
		event_target:tradecountry = {
			capital_scope = {
				set_province_flag = traderwife_here
				add_province_modifier = { 
					name = cosmo_trader_wife
					duration = 10950 
				}				
			}
		}		
		trigger_switch = {
		on_trigger = has_ruler_flag					
		african_trader_wives = {
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africantrader_events.30
			}
			}
		sa_trader_wives = {
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_satrader_events.30
			}
			}
		na_trader_wives = {
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_natrader_events.30
			}
			}												
		}
		clr_ruler_flag = african_trader_wives		
		clr_ruler_flag = sa_trader_wives		
		clr_ruler_flag = na_trader_wives		
		set_ruler_flag = traderwife_possible		
	}
	option = {#owned provinces
		name = cosmo_africantrader_events.21.b
		trigger = {
		event_target:tradecountry = {
			any_owned_province = {
				has_owner_culture = yes
				NOT = {
					trade_goods = fish 
					trade_goods = wool 
					trade_goods = grain 
					trade_goods = livestock 
					trade_goods = naval_supplies 
				}
				NOT = {
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_trader_wife
				}
			}
		}			
		}
		event_target:tradecountry = {
			every_owned_province = {
				limit = {
				has_owner_culture = yes
				NOT = {
					trade_goods = fish 
					trade_goods = wool 
					trade_goods = grain 
					trade_goods = livestock 
					trade_goods = naval_supplies 
				}
				OR = {
					base_production = 1
				}								
				NOT = {
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_trader_wife
				}					
				}
				set_province_flag = traderwife_possible				
			}
		}		
		trigger_switch = {#province list owned
		on_trigger = has_ruler_flag					
		african_trader_wives = {
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africantrader_events.20
			}			
		}
		sa_trader_wives = {
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_satrader_events.20
			}
		}
		na_trader_wives = {
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_natrader_events.20
			}
			}												
		}
		clr_ruler_flag = african_trader_wives		
		clr_ruler_flag = sa_trader_wives		
		clr_ruler_flag = na_trader_wives							
	}
	option = {#empty provinces
		name = cosmo_africantrader_events.21.c
		trigger = {
		event_target:tradecountry = {
			any_province = {
				is_empty = yes 
				culture = PREV
				has_discovered = ROOT
				native_size = 0.5
				NOT = {
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_trader_wife
				}
			}
		}			
		}
		event_target:tradecountry = {
			every_province = {
				limit = {
				is_empty = yes 
				culture = PREV
				native_size = 0.5
				has_discovered = ROOT
				NOT = {
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_trader_wife
				}					
				}
				set_province_flag = traderwife_possible				
			}
		}		
		trigger_switch = {#province list empty
		on_trigger = has_ruler_flag					
		african_trader_wives = {
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_africantrader_events.20
			}		
		}
		sa_trader_wives = {
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_satrader_events.20
			}
			}
		na_trader_wives = {
		country_event = {
			id = cosmo_natrader_events.20
			}
			}												
		}
		clr_ruler_flag = african_trader_wives		
		clr_ruler_flag = sa_trader_wives		
		clr_ruler_flag = na_trader_wives							
	}
	option = {#none available
		name = cosmo_africantrader_events.21.7
			hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = cosmo_trader_wives_offer
					}					
				}
				remove_opinion = {
					who = ROOT
					modifier = cosmo_trader_wives_offer
				}				
			}
			}		
		clr_ruler_flag = african_trader_wives		
		clr_ruler_flag = sa_trader_wives		
		clr_ruler_flag = na_trader_wives							
	}
	after = {
		clr_country_flag = cosmo_traderwife_relaunch
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = cosmo_traderwife_repeat
			}
			clr_country_flag = cosmo_traderwife_repeat
		}
	}																	
}

				}
				}			
			}
		NOT = {
			has_country_flag = cosmo_traderwife_relaunch
		}			
	}

	
	immediate = {
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_country_flag = cosmo_trader_wives_offered
				random_known_country = {
				limit = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT
						modifier = cosmo_trader_wives_offer
					}
				}
					save_event_target_as = tradecountry
					capital_scope = {

		trigger = {

			capital_scope = {
				NOT = {
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_trader_wife
				}
			}

		name = cosmo_africantrader_events.2.b
		trigger = {
		event_target:tradecountry = {
			any_owned_province = {
				has_owner_culture = yes
				NOT = {
					trade_goods = fish 
					trade_goods = wool 
					trade_goods = grain 
					trade_goods = livestock 
					trade_goods = naval_supplies

		event_target:tradecountry = {
			every_owned_province = {
				limit = {
				has_owner_culture = yes
				NOT = {
					trade_goods = fish 
					trade_goods = wool 
					trade_goods = grain 
					trade_goods = livestock 
					trade_goods = naval_supplies 
				}

					base_production = 1
				}

				has_province_modifier = cosmo_trader_wife
				}					
				}

	}

				limit = {
					has_opinion_modifier = {
						who = ROOT

					}					
				}

					who = ROOT
					modifier = cosmo_trader_wives_offer
				}				
			}
			}		
		clr_ruler_flag = african_trader_wives		
		clr_ruler_flag = sa_trader_wives		
		clr_ruler_flag = na_trader_wives							
	}

			which = cosmoAWivesThreeBCounter	
			value = 400
			}

			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}

		}

			value = 400
			}
			}

		}

			which = cosmoAWivesSevenBCounter	
			value = 280
			}

			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor

			}

		}

			value = 280
			}
			}

		which = cosmoAWivesSevenBCounter

			}
	}

	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_12C_6_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {

	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoAWivesTwelveCCounter
			value = 241
			}
	}

	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_12C_2_eventPicture
	}

			which = cosmoAWivesTwelveCCounter

			}

	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_12C_4_eventPicture #2nd
	}

	trigger = {
	NOT = {	
	check_variable = {
			which = cosmoAWivesTwelveCCounter
			value = 281
			}
	}	
	}
	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_12C_5_eventPicture #2nd
	}
	goto = traderprovince
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
	
	}


	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_12c
			}
					save_event_target_as = traderprovince		
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = traderwife_here
				culture = FROM
			}
					save_event_target_as = traderprovince		
		}																
		set_ruler_flag = traderwife_possible																
		}
	}
	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_africantrader_events.1230.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = traderwife_possible	
			}
			trader_wives_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoAfricanCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesTwelveCCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoAWivesTwelveCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_kyushuan_theologian
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoAWivesTwelveCCounter	
			value = 281
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesTwelveCCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}	
}

			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesThirteenACounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}		   		
	}

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoAfricanCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}

		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesThirteenCCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoAWivesThirteenCCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_kyushuan_theologian
		}		
		if = {

			value = 281
			}
			}
		set_variable = {

		value = 0
			}

	picture = cosmogfx_WIVES_AFRICA_MENU_14B_1_eventPicture

	goto = traderprovince
	is_triggered_only = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
	
	}

	immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
		random_province = {
			limit = {
			has_province_modifier = africa_concubine_14b
			}
					save_event_target_as = traderprovince		
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				has_province_flag = traderwife_here
				culture = FROM
			}
					save_event_target_as = traderprovince		
		}																
		set_ruler_flag = traderwife_possible																
		}
	}

	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_africantrader_events.1420.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_ruler_flag = traderwife_possible	
			}
			trader_wives_effect = yes
		   }
		hidden_effect = {

			change_variable = {
				which = cosmoAfricanCounter
				value = 1
				}					   
			}		   		   		   		   		   		   		   
		hidden_effect = {
		change_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesFourteenBCounter
		value = 20
		}
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoAWivesFourteenBCounter	
			value = 280
			}
		NOT = {
			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		}	
			}	
		set_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor
		set_ruler_flag = harem_togoku_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoAWivesFourteenBCounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesFourteenBCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}	
}

			value = 280
			}

			has_ruler_flag = has_harem_advisor

			}

		set_ruler_flag = harem_togoku_statesman
		}		
		if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
			which = cosmoAWivesFifteenBCounter	
			value = 280
			}
			}
		set_variable = {
		which = cosmoAWivesFifteenBCounter
		value = 0
			}
		}
	}	
	}
